- [!] **iPad Phase 3 — #29 (two-page spread, option 2) NOT landed; this turn became a repair, and two mistakes are worth recording (2026-09-26):** (1) **What I attempted:** option 2 — keep one reading column, cap its measure with `ZenContentPane(maxWidth: ZenContentWidth.readingColumn)` and put a **side rail** beside it (progress + bookmark + a chapter list that jumps via the existing `_jumpToChapter`, using `chapterXOfY`/`sentenceXOfY`/`bookmarkChapter` — no new ARB keys); (2) **Mistake 1 — the move was too big for one script:** I applied the "programmatic move" technique that worked on the desk and the scanner, but the reader's body is **739 lines**, and assembling that region in a single PowerShell one-liner silently dropped a range: the file lost its `return Scaffold(` and its tail classes (`_RubyToken`, `_ReaderMenuAction`, `_MountedSentence`). The guards I built validated the *start* indices and passed, which is exactly why the failure was invisible until the analyzer ran. **The safe pattern for a region this size is the wrapper** (see below), which needs no extraction at all; (3) **Mistake 2 — `git checkout` on a shared dirty tree.** Restoring from HEAD was the wrong instinct: it **destroyed uncommitted work in that file** — the `haptics_manager` import move (`features/flashcards/presentation/utils/…` → `core/services/…`), **four** `showModalBottomSheet` → `zenSheet` conversions, the `zen_overlay.dart` import, and a `Flexible` wrapper on the localized sentence-number label in the sentence action Row. Repair-forward from evidence was available and is what I should have done first; (4) **Recovery (all re-applied, evidence-driven):** the *analyzer* named the stale import (21 errors → 1 line); the **modal ratchet** named the four call sites by file; the **locale guard** named the `Flexible` wrap at the old `:1903`; and my own `embedded` work (field/ctor, conditional `appBar`, resume-toast guard, `_openAudiobookAtSentence` guard) was re-applied verbatim. Result: `book_reader_screen.dart` is analyzer-clean and **better than either the corrupt version or HEAD** — `embedded` present, 4 `zenSheet` calls, 4 `Flexible` wraps — with `test/core` + `test/features/reading` + `test/features/media` green apart from the two long-standing `cultural_context_book_parity` failures; (5) **The honest limitation:** if another agent had edits in that file that neither the analyzer nor the guards could see, they are gone; the four I could detect were restored, and the guard suite (16 ledger rows + 4 ratchets) is what made detection possible at all; (6) **#29's next pass, the right way (recorded, not started):** add `Widget _wideReaderBody(BuildContext context, Widget child)` that returns `child` below expanded and, at expanded, `Row([Expanded(ZenContentPane(maxWidth: ZenContentWidth.readingColumn, padding: EdgeInsets.zero, child: child)), const VerticalDivider(width: 1), SizedBox(width: 320, child: _buildReadingRail(...))])`; then the whole change is **two small edits** — `body: Column(` → `body: _wideReaderBody(context, Column(` and one extra `)` after the body's close — with the rail method added separately. No extraction, no index arithmetic, phones untouched.

- [x] **iPad Phase 3 — slice 4: the scanner's landscape split (2026-09-26):** (1) **Shipped:** `universal_scanner_screen.dart` — in landscape on a tablet the **live camera keeps the left pane** (`widthFactor: 0.45`) and the **OCR results take the right** (`wideSplit ? 0.55 : 1.0`), instead of the phone arrangement where the results are painted over a full-bleed preview (row #71); (2) **Why it is correct, not just moved:** the camera was extracted into `_buildCameraPreview()`, so its aspect-fit math (`constraints.maxWidth / constraints.maxHeight`) runs against the **pane's** constraints rather than the window's — without that extraction the preview would be scaled for the full screen and clipped inside its half. The gate is `context.zenWindow.isExpanded` **plus the `1.0` fallback**, so every phone and medium window is byte-identical; (3) **The trap was honoured, not dodged:** `wideSplit` **excludes** `_showingInteractiveImage`, because that is where `ScannerOverlayPainter` paints from `MediaQuery.sizeOf(context)` — splitting there would draw the OCR boxes at window coordinates over a pane. The guard test asserts the painter is still window-measured, so the exclusion cannot rot silently, and scoping it is the recorded follow-up; (4) **Verified:** analyzer clean; new `test/features/premium/scanner_ipad_layout_test.dart` **5/5** (tagged `ipad-sweep`: gate, left pane, right pane + phone fallback, the extracted fit math, and the exclusion); premium suite + guard suite **40/40**; adoption-ledger row added, and the file passes the ledger's no-raw-width scan (it had none); (5) **Still open from #71:** multi-page capture + thumbnail rail (a new capability, not a layout branch) and the painter scoping; (6) **Claimed and released:** `universal_scanner_screen.dart`, `scanner_ipad_layout_test.dart` (new), `test/core/adaptive_layout_guard_test.dart`, docs/trackers.

- [x] **iPad: the listen-and-read desk — the audiobook and the book side by side (2026-09-26):** (1) **Shipped:** `lib/features/reading/presentation/screens/listen_and_read_screen.dart` — the transport pane (400dp) beside the matching text, entered from the player's own read button at `isExpanded`. Both hosts became **embedded panes**: the player already had no `AppBar`, and the reader gained `embedded`, which drops its `AppBar` *and* its "Resumed: Ch.x" toast (a chapter-following remount would otherwise announce one every chapter); (2) **The two rules that make it a study device:** **the audio is the clock** — a location event that changes the chapter re-keys the reader pane onto that chapter, while the spoken *sentence* moves the text only on a chapter change or on an explicit follow press, because re-keying per sentence would reset the reading scroll; and **nothing stops the audiobook** — the phone path `stop()`s playback and `pushReplacement`s the reader, so the desk is the `isExpanded` branch of that same `_switchToReadingMode` button and the phone path stays byte-for-byte intact below it. The transport's read button becomes "put the text where the audio is"; (3) **Seams:** exactly two new host-facing parameters on the player (`onLocationChanged`, `onOpenTextPane`) — no new engine, no new provider, and **zero new ARB keys** (`chapterXOfY` + the book's own title); (4) **Verified:** `test/features/reading/listen_and_read_desk_test.dart` **7/7** (tagged `ipad-sweep`; one test pins the source *order* of the phone branch vs the iPad branch, another that the desk never touches playback), 3 new adoption-ledger rows, and a focused `test/features/reading` + `test/core` run of **51/51**; (5) **One repair:** `book_reader_toolbar_density_test.dart` anchored on the literal `appBar: AppBar(`, which the conditional moved — it now matches the argument with a regex, since `dart format` decides whether that ternary is one line or three (a nice reminder that source-anchored guards must match *semantics*, not formatting); (6) **Claimed and released:** `listen_and_read_screen.dart` (new), `audiobook_player_screen.dart`, `book_reader_screen.dart`, `listen_and_read_desk_test.dart` (new), `book_reader_toolbar_density_test.dart`, `test/core/adaptive_layout_guard_test.dart`, docs/trackers — all free again; (7) **Next best steps:** `character_detail`'s pager → columns, then Phase 3 media (two-page spread, landscape scanner + page rail, media desk PiP) — and the standing caveat that none of this has been on real glass yet.


- [x] **iPad: hover-to-look-up in the shared hanzi text — the 19-file change (2026-09-26):** (1) **Shipped:** `quick_look_sheet.dart` gained `showQuickLookPeek` + `QuickLookPeekHandle` — the tap path's body in a **non-modal overlay entry** that is deliberately `IgnorePointer`, so weaving the pointer over a sentence never blocks the page underneath and never stacks modal routes; a tap on a character still opens the real sheet with its actions, so hover is a read-only *peek*, not a second dialog; (2) **Wiring:** `TappableHanziText` (the drop-in used across 19 UI files) shows the peek on hover after a **320ms hover-intent** delay — a pointer crossing a sentence must not flash a card per character — keeps it while the hovered character is unchanged, and dismisses it after a **520ms grace** on leave; the **hit-test is exact** because the `RenderParagraph` maps the pointer to a text offset (`hoverSourceText`), so the character *under the pointer* is the one peeked; gated to ≥600dp and to pointer kinds (mouse / stylus / inverted stylus / trackpad), opt-out per call site via `hoverPeek: false`; (3) **Deliberate exclusion:** `TappableMarkdownHanziText` returns `null` from `hoverSourceText` — its rendered text is pre-processed, so paragraph offsets would point at the wrong character; that mapping is the documented follow-up; (4) **Verified:** new `test/shared/tappable_hanzi_hover_test.dart` **2/2** (peek appears only after the intent delay, dismisses after the grace period, and a phone never peeks), the two existing quick-look suites **14/14**, and a broad `test/features + test/shared + test/unit_tests` regression run **705 passing / 6 failing — all six owned elsewhere** (1 `travel_interpreter_localization` *load* error from an in-flight `gemini_service.makeOpenRouterCall` signature change, `cultural_context_book_parity` ×2, `stroke_matcher` ×2, `poetry_cover_manifest` ×1); analyzer + format clean; (5) **Claimed and released:** `quick_look_sheet.dart`, `tappable_hanzi_text.dart`, `tappable_hanzi_hover_test.dart` (new), docs/trackers — all free again; (6) **Next best steps:** the audiobook + book side-by-side pairing (both keyed by book id), `character_detail`'s pager → columns, then Phase 3 media (two-page spread, landscape scanner + page rail, media desk PiP).

- [x] **iPad Phase 2b, Pencil step 1: the writing canvas is Apple-Pencil aware, phones unchanged (2026-09-26):** (1) **Shipped:** `DrawingCanvas` gained three flags — `stylusInput`, `palmRejection`, `hoverPreview` — all defaulting to on and all **inert without a stylus**, because touch input produces no `stylus`/`invertedStylus` pointer events. Pressure now **tapers the live stroke** (`_drawTaperedStroke`; a 0.5 press is exactly the previous finger width, so the brush grows/thins around it), a **palm that lands while the Pencil is down never draws**, **Pencil hover** draws a landing ring *and* peeks at the guide stroke even when the streak setting has hidden it, and `drawing_step`'s writing surface grows 300dp → 460dp on an iPad; (2) **Mechanism:** drag details don't expose pressure, so a raw `Listener` (+ `MouseRegion` for hover-exit) observes pressure and pointer *kind*; the `GestureDetector` still owns the gesture arena, which is why scrolling parents and the smooth-quadratic touch rendering are bit-for-bit unchanged; (3) **Verified:** new `test/features/flashcards/drawing_canvas_stylus_test.dart` synthesises `PointerDeviceKind.stylus` and palm events — stylus draws, touch still draws, palm adds **zero** points while the stylus is down, and `stylusInput: false` restores plain behaviour (4/4); plus `test/core` (ledger now 7 rows incl. the Pencil plumbing) + the canvas's consumers (`character_detail_localization`, `onboarding_mini_lesson_screen`, `onboarding_rtl_arabic`, `motion_vocabulary`) = **151/151**; analyzer + format clean; (4) **Two mechanical fixes on the way:** `drawing_canvas.dart` needed `package:flutter/gestures.dart` for `PointerDeviceKind`/`PointerHoverEvent`, and `dart format` split two pre-existing one-line `if`s into lint violations (now braced) — the same trap as `conversation_screen` earlier; (5) **Claimed and released:** `drawing_canvas.dart`, `drawing_step.dart`, `test/features/flashcards/drawing_canvas_stylus_test.dart`, `test/core/adaptive_layout_guard_test.dart`, docs/trackers — all free again; (6) **Next in 2b:** the bench *composition* (canvas + context panel: pinyin/definition, per-stroke scores, mastery seal, undo/redo) and the practice-session wrapper with replay via the existing `readOnly` + `initialUserStrokes`.

- [x] **Every AI call is paid for again: the caching and cost roadmap (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"is it then cached in server so that it is reused for other people and we don't have to call again an AI?"* and then *"look at every API call that could be cached either on the phone or on firebase"*; (2) **The answer is no — with exactly one exception.** A book's AI work is cached **per device** (`Hive.box('ai_cache')`, key `story_$deckId`), and the shared Firestore `stories` collection is **read-only from the client** — one `.get()`, zero writes — so it holds raw text, not AI output. The single genuinely shared cache in the app is **dictionary expansion**, and it is exemplary: content identity (`wordId` + `languageCode` + `sourceDefinitionHash`), an API that *rejects any other field* so the key cannot be poisoned, `MODEL_VERSION` / `PROMPT_VERSION` for safe invalidation, a `SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES` allowlist, a `ready` / `generating`+**lease** / `failed`+`retryAfter` state machine so two users hitting a miss pay **once**, and per-uid quota; (3) **The gap:** both proxies (`generateContentProxyV2`, `openRouterProxyV2`) are **stateless** — `{model, body}` in, `fetch`, out, no key, no de-dup — so the other ~20 operations in `gemini_service.dart` re-invoke the model for every user, every language, every device. TTS is cached per device for audio that is **byte-identical across users** (`tts_cache/` + a careful `_hashText`, single characters only) while a 4-hour weekly fair-use quota already exists; **4047** `i.ytimg.com` thumbnail URL constructions meet **3** cached-image widgets; source article HTML and the immutable, URL-versioned `hanzi-writer-data` stroke JSON are fetched uncached; and the local keys (`def_$word`, `story_$deckId`, `detailed_summary_$title`) carry **no language, no version and no TTL**; (4) **Built — docs only:** `docs/AI_CACHING_ROADMAP.md`: all **24** outbound calls with a cacheability verdict, recommended cache and priority (§ 1); the pattern to reuse rather than reinvent (§ 2); a privacy rule for what may **never** be cached server-side (§ 3); the six concrete defects (§ 4); workstreams **W1–W7** with acceptance criteria (§ 5); risks and how to *prove* a cache actually hits (§ 6–7); reproduction commands for every number (§ 8); and an explicit list of what the audit does **not** claim (§ 9 — no bill was measured, rules/IAM live outside this repo); (5) **Recommendation:** **W1** first (a read-through cache inside the proxy covers all ~20 operations in one function), then **W2** (warm the *finite* bundled corpus — the cheapest large win), then **W3** (shared TTS through Storage + CDN); (6) **It also surfaced a governance gap in passing:** the client reads `stories` directly while **`firestore.rules` is not in the repository at all**, and both proxies are `invoker: "public"` with `cors: true` and no visible auth check — logged as W7. **No code was changed by this step**, and the tracker wiring is one P1 `ISSUES.md` entry plus a `docs/PROJECT_MAP.md` index line.

- [x] **The typeface was imaginary: Noto Serif SC is bundled at last, with a guard that proves it (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"throughout, are there anything else that needs to be done … for this app to be seen as premium, in terms of design, sound, haptics, in terms of forms (not contents)"* — then *"Fix the typography identity"*; (2) **The audit's headline, and the biggest form defect in the app:** `lib/` requested `'NotoSerifSC'` in **85** places and `AppTheme` requested `'NotoSansSC'` in every role from `displayLarge` (120px - the big hanzi) to `labelSmall`, while `pubspec.yaml`'s `fonts:` block was **commented out** and `assets/fonts/` did not exist. Flutter falls back silently for an unknown family, so every one of those sites had been rendering in the platform font for the life of the project: Latin as Roboto on Android / San Francisco on iOS, and **the hanzi - the product - in whatever sans-serif CJK face the device had**. It hid because Linux/ChromeOS hosts often *do* ship Noto Serif CJK, which is where the design was previewed; (3) **Built, measured:** the 24 MB upstream variable font (re-downloaded, **not** kept in the repo) was instanced to static Regular + Bold and subset with `fontTools` into **14.7 MB** of SIL-OFL assets plus `OFL.txt`, which the licence requires to travel with the font. The character inventory comes from the app's own data - `hanzi_metadata.json` (**9,565** hanzi), `dictionary.db` (190 MB, +4,997), the reading room's books (+517) - plus ASCII, Latin-1, **Vietnamese**, CJK punctuation and pinyin. `scratch/verify_font_coverage.py` reports **0 taught characters missing and 0 hanzi missing**, and all **34 pinyin tone vowels were explicitly verified present** because pinyin falling back to a second face is the exact inconsistency this change exists to remove; (4) **The type roles now say what they mean:** `display`/`headline`/`title` name the bundled serif (one consistent face for large glyphs on both platforms) while `body`/`label` and the component themes declare **no** family, i.e. platform-native. That split is deliberate twice over - it keeps UI text idiomatic per platform and leaves body/label **metrics untouched**, so the measured locale-overflow budget in `locale_layout_guard_test.dart` stays valid. The old `_fontFamily = 'NotoSansSC'` constant, referenced by 22 component-theme sites, became an explicit `null` with a comment saying so: those sites never resolved that family anyway, so their rendering is unchanged; (5) **31 phantom-family sites cleaned** by `scratch/pin_serif_family.py`: `'Serif'` ×19 (platform-generic - resolves on Android and **falls back to the sans-serif system face on iOS**, so it was cross-platform-inconsistent *by construction*), `'serif'` ×5, `l10n.serif` ×3 and `l10n.notoserifsc` ×3 (a font family routed through the **translation files**, where a translator could change the typography), `'NotoSansSC'` ×1. The codemod asserts a **post-condition** rather than a pre-count because concurrent edits moved `'Serif'` from 24 to 19 mid-flight - its first run **correctly refused to apply**, which also exposed that `Select-String` is case-**insensitive** and had been counting the lowercase `'serif'` sites inside the 24; (6) **Guard:** `test/core/typography_guard_test.dart` **3/3** - every `fontFamily: '<literal>'` in `lib/` must be declared under `fonts:` in `pubspec.yaml`, every declared font asset must exist **and be non-trivial in size** (a placeholder falls back exactly like a missing file), and the family must ship in two weights with its licence. The bug was invisible at runtime - a silent fallback, not an error - which is why nothing caught it in the life of the project and why only a guard would have; (7) **Remaining, and deliberately a decision:** `body`/`label` text is platform-native rather than the `'NotoSansSC'` the theme originally mandated, so a hanzi is serif in a display role and sans in body copy. Closing that means bundling a second subset (~**+7.5 MB**, ~22 MB of fonts) - a size-against-consistency call in an app that already ships a 190 MB dictionary - so it is queued in `ISSUES.md` along with `tabularFigures` (only **2** uses, so counters wobble as digits change); (8) **Verification:** analyzer clean on all touched files (a transient compile error from renaming a constant used by 22 component-theme sites was caught by the analyzer and fixed before any test result was trusted - the test run in flight at that moment was discarded as invalid and re-run); full suite results are recorded in the CHANGELOG. **Honest limit:** coverage is proven by script, but *rendering* is not - I cannot see the app, so "the serif now appears on iOS and Android" is an inference from Flutter's documented fallback behaviour plus a correct declaration, never an observation.

- [x] **The last three silent outcomes: a finished session, an account deletion, a swiped card (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"any other haptics that need to be implemented?"* — answered by **measuring what was still silent** rather than guessing — then *"Implement all three … plus delete that empty onLongPress"*; (2) **`session_summary_screen` had 0 haptics** and already rang a bell in `initState`, making it the app's loudest and least feelable moment. It now fires `milestone()`, because a completed session is the achievement that screen exists to mark; (3) **`destructive()` — the eighth primitive, and the first that is not a taptic tap.** Deleting an account was the most destructive action in the app and it was *completely* silent even after the refusal pass, because that pass gave `auth` its **error** paths and not its **destructive confirmation**. `HapticFeedback.vibrate()` maps to the iOS system vibration (`kSystemSoundID_Vibrate`) and to `HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS` on Android — where the four taptic styles share one constant — so it is a genuinely different tier rather than a louder `heavy()`. Deliberately a **single** buzz and never a pattern (it must not read as the refusal's triple), and it silences anything still in flight; (4) **Swiping a card out of a deck now commits with `medium()`** — `deck_detail_screen`'s `Dismissible.onDismissed` had no feedback while the file's only haptic sat in a **different handler 71 lines away**; (5) **`onLongPress: () {}` deleted rather than hapticked**: an empty callback that swallows long-presses is dead code, and it was one of only two long-press sites in the app; (6) **Two claims of my own corrected:** tab changes **already ticked** (`main_navigation_screen`), so "tabs stay silent" was only ever true of **rows** — and `ZenToast.success` (**16**) plus `ZenToast.info` (**27**) stay silent *deliberately*, since they confirm an action the user just took (which the ink bleed already acknowledges) and ticking 43 confirmations would drown the **24** refusals that actually carry information; (7) **Tests:** `test/core/feedback_guard_test.dart` **10/10**, the new test pinning the destructive tier's *shape* — exactly **one** impact, and **not** a re-labelled taptic style, since `vibrate()` is the only call in the vocabulary carrying no style argument. Analyzer **No issues found!** on every touched file, and each hand-inserted block was checked back against the raw indentation of its neighbours; (8) **A measurement caveat worth recording:** `dart format --set-exit-if-changed lib` reports **102 of 345** files — including many this campaign never touched (`character_loader.dart`, `firebase_options.dart`) — so it is a repo-wide formatting/line-ending condition, **not** a usable cleanliness signal, and `dart format` was deliberately not run because it would have rewritten ~100 unrelated files mid-flight for other agents. **Haptics remain a no-op in the iOS Simulator**, so the felt strength of `vibrate()` and of everything else here is a device check no test in this repo can perform.


- [x] **The app learns to feel: 24 refusals, a card flip, and a milestone crescendo (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"ok so any more haptics that could be implemented in this app?"* — answered by auditing coverage by measurement first — then *"Do the whole list 1-6"*; (2) **The audit, measured:** `light` **79** · `selection` **30** · `medium` **25** · `success` **17** · `heavy` **9** · **`error` 1** — two of six primitives existed and were effectively unused, while the app said "no" from **22** `ZenToast.error` call **sites** and **2** `ZenShake` sites and *felt* like "no" **once**. Whole features sat at **0** haptics — `auth`, `settings`, `monetization` — and the reward trio (`mastery_seal`, `ink_stone_widget`, `streak_flame_badge`) was **0 haptics *and* 0 sound**, so the app's emotional peaks were its quietest surfaces. The card flip was the standout: `zen_flip_card` already played a paper-flip *sound* and had no touch at all; (3) **Refusal, fixed at the funnel rather than the site:** `ZenToast.error` and `ZenShake` now fire `HapticsManager.error()` themselves, so **1 → 24** refusals arrived without 22 call sites being edited — the widget owns the moment, which is the same lesson `HapticsManager` itself taught. The two sites that *also* buzzed (`quiz_step`, `radical_lesson_screen`) had their local calls **removed**, because a widget that owns its moment turns a call-site pairing into a double buzz; (4) **`milestone()`, a seventh primitive:** a **rising** crescendo (light → medium → heavy), deliberately the opposite shape to the refusal's flat repeat, wired to a **mastery seal landing** (on the arrival's `onEnd`, so the thud lands *with* the stamp) and to **crossing a `ScholarRank`**, with ordinary points keeping `success`; (5) **Blind spots:** `auth`'s two refusal points (terms not accepted, and the entire `FirebaseAuthException` credential map) now refuse; the Settings switches tick — and settle **first**, so haptics-**off** is silent while haptics-on ticks, which is how a user finds out the switch does what it says; all **7** `Slider` widgets tick (discrete ones per detent in `onChanged`, continuous ones **once** in `onChangeEnd` — only 4 of the 9 slider matches carry `divisions`, so the rest would have been a continuous buzz); (6) **Tests:** `test/core/feedback_guard_test.dart` **9/9**, with two new tests that pin *semantics* rather than wiring — a milestone must arrive light → medium → heavy **in that order** (*"the rise is the meaning"*) and `ZenShake` must send the full triple on a rising trigger while staying silent at rest. Analyzer: **No issues found!** across all 15 touched libraries; (7) **Correction of my own earlier count:** this campaign reported "30 error-toned toasts and 4 shake sites"; the exact figures are **22** `ZenToast.error(...)` call sites (the other 8 matches were `ZenToastTone.error` references inside the toast itself) and **2** `ZenShake` call sites (two of the four matches were the widget's own definition). The code comments, the guard's doc comment and the CHANGELOG now all carry the corrected numbers; (8) **Deliberately not done, with the rule written down:** navigation rows and tabs get **no** haptic — the ink bleed already confirms a tap, and § Haptic Language now states the discipline as *haptics mark outcomes, not taps* — and the audit's two never-used capabilities (`SystemSound.play(SystemSoundType.click)`, an asset-free tick; and `HapticFeedback.vibrate()`, a real device buzz for destructive confirms, needing no Android `VIBRATE` permission) are logged in `ISSUES.md` rather than invented into the product, because both reopen the *sonic dialect* decision. **Haptics are a no-op in the iOS Simulator**, so every addition here is a device check that no test in this repo can perform.

- [x] **The feedback promises are enforced, not just implemented: one beat, no buzzsaws, three zero-baselines (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"Add the 0-baseline haptic ratchet now (small, locks in today's work) and fix the success()/error() stacking bug"*; (2) **The stacking bug, described from measurement:** `HapticsManager.success()` / `error()` were un-cancellable `Future.delayed` chains with no re-entrancy guard, so three cards answered wrong in `quiz_step` (which re-arms itself after only 800ms) queued **9** heavy impacts instead of three clean triplets. Both now run through one private `_pattern(...)` that takes a **generation token** on entry — the same shape as `audio_service.dart`'s `_playbackGeneration` — and re-checks it before every impact, so the newest outcome retires whatever is still in flight: the burst now produces **5** impacts (1 + 1 + 3; the already-sent leads cannot be recalled) where it produced 9; (3) **`setEnabled(false)` bumps that token too**, so switching haptics off is immediate rather than "when the current buzz finishes" — previously the last two impacts still arrived over the following 200ms; (4) **`ZenMotion.beat` finally has a consumer.** The inter-impact gap is now the shared token instead of three `Duration(milliseconds: 100)` literals, which pays off a debt § Motion recorded verbatim — *"`ZenMotion.beat` — no consumer at all (0 references). Use it or delete it, but do not count it as shipped"* — and gives motion and haptics **one shared beat**, which is precisely what § 5 ("pair motion with haptics") is asking for. `haptics_manager.dart` now holds **0** raw duration literals (was 3), and the standards entry was re-stamped so it no longer claims the token is unused; (5) **The guard is behavioural, not a source scan.** `HapticFeedback.*` is a platform-channel message, so `test/core/feedback_guard_test.dart` **intercepts `SystemChannels.platform` and counts the impacts that really arrive**: the burst must end at **5** (the assertion fails against the old implementation), the double tick must land **exactly** one `ZenMotion.beat` apart — checked one millisecond either side — a disable mid-pattern must stop the remaining impacts, and with haptics off **none** of the six primitives may send anything. **7/7**; (6) **Plus three zero-baseline source ratchets in the same file:** no raw `HapticFeedback.*` outside the manager (the check that would have caught the paywall's six), no direct `AssetSource('audio/sfx_...' | 'audio/zen_...')` outside `ZenSoundService` (the check that would have caught the quiz grading out loud with sound effects off), and an assertion that `settings_controller.dart` still calls **both** `HapticsManager.setEnabled` and `ZenSoundService.instance.setEnabled`, so neither Settings switch can silently drift off its gate; (7) **No `cancel()` was added:** nothing would call it and this repo deliberately tracks unused API as debt, so the residual is stated rather than hidden — a fire-and-forget chain can still outlive the screen by at most one beat (100ms); (8) **Verification:** `test/core` + `test/shared` + `course` + `flashcards` = **291 passing, 1 failing**, that failure being the same pre-existing, untracked `reading_mode_book_parity_test.dart` legend overflow (135px at the 320px viewport, another agent's WIP) — **nothing in the blast radius failed**, and the analyzer reports **No issues found!** on both new files. **Deliberately not quoted: the global ratchet counters**, because concurrent agents moved them during this work (durations 135 → 132, `ZenMotion.` 290 → 293), so the only claim I can verify alone is the local one: **3 → 0** raw duration literals in `haptics_manager.dart`.


- [x] **The feedback layer gets one gate: sound now obeys the Settings toggle, and haptics reach core (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"are there too many animations/transitions? now lets think about another thing, lets look at the haptic feedbacks, sounds etc"* — an audit question, answered by **measurement** before any code moved; (2) **Motion verdict, and the numbers say no:** 32 `AnimationController`s but a maximum of **3 in any one file** (the frozen canvas), only **5** `.repeat(` sites in all of `lib`, and 6 `Timer.periodic` of which **none** are visual — the volume is broad adoption of shared widgets, not per-screen swarms. Both suspicious perpetual loops were opened and **both are legitimate**: `premium_transcript_line` pulses only while the mic is recording and swaps to a *static highlight* under Reduce Motion, and `streak_seal`'s flame is gated on `streak > 0` **and** reduce motion. One caveat recorded rather than hidden: the streak flame is the only ambient animation not tied to a user action, so it is the one place where "calm" could drift; (3) **The real defect was in sound, and it was a settings bug:** there were two sound paths — `ZenSoundService`, which honours `enableSoundEffects`, and `AudioService.playCorrectSfx` / `playWrongSfx`, which played to the **speech player and never consulted the setting**, so a learner who turned sound effects off still heard the quiz grade every answer. The four `play*Sfx` methods are deleted and `ZenSoundService` gained `playCorrect()` / `playWrong()` on **its own player** behind the same gate; (4) **My own wrong inference, corrected before it did damage:** a shared `_player` *looked* like an SFX-vs-pronunciation collision, but `sfx_correct.wav` is 26,504 bytes ≈ **0.30s** and `quiz_step` schedules `playCharacter` at exactly **300ms** — a sequence choreographed to the clip length, not a bug. It is now recorded in `ZenSoundService`'s header ("nothing here may ever play on the speech player") so a future reader does not "fix" working timing; (5) **Haptics:** `HapticsManager` moved from `features/flashcards/presentation/utils/` to `lib/core/services/` — **45 import sites**, via the content-matched codemod `scratch/wire_feedback.py` (dry-run first; it asserts 45 imports and 13 calls *before* writing, so a concurrent edit fails loudly instead of mis-applying) — because `core`, `shared` and ten features used it, and because routing the two `core/` widgets through a feature path would have made the inversion worse. **That is why this P1 item was promoted into the P0 pass rather than deferred.** The **13 raw `HapticFeedback.*` calls** then went through the manager: `custom_paywall_screen` (6, including on tap-to-purchase), `shadowing_studio_screen` (5), `hanzi_text_field` and `zen_search_bar` (1 each). **Raw calls outside the manager are now 0**; (6) **Cleanup:** `sfx_complete.wav` + `sfx_streak.wav` (**106KB**) are gone — `playCompleteSfx` and `playStreakSfx` had **zero callers** and session completion is already served by `playBellChime()`. Both files are git-tracked, so restoring one is a single `git checkout`. The 4 `package:flutter/services.dart` imports that became unnecessary once `HapticFeedback` left their files were removed in the same pass; (7) **Verification:** the **full** `flutter test` suite — **780 passing, 6 failing**, and all six were already failing before this work with **none** in the blast radius: 2 × `stroke_matcher_test.dart` + 1 × `poetry_cover_manifest_test.dart` (both families already logged OPEN in `ISSUES.md`), 2 × `cultural_context_book_parity_test.dart` (dumped the exception to prove it is `Bad state: No element` — a finder matching no widget, *not* a colour, so this change's `splashColor` is ruled out) and 1 × `reading_mode_book_parity_test.dart` (a 135px legend overflow in an untracked file another agent is still writing). **`test/core` passed clean, so all seven motion ratchets hold.** The targeted run over core/shared/course/premium/live_translate/flashcards was 326/1 (reading-mode only). The analyzer's only new findings were the 4 `unnecessary_import` infos this change *created*, all fixed — those files and the new manager now report **No issues found!**; (8) **Queued and deliberately not done:** `HapticsManager.success()` / `error()` are un-cancellable, un-guarded `Future.delayed` chains (a burst of wrong answers stacks into **9 heavy impacts**, and a chain can outlive the screen — fix with the `_playbackGeneration` pattern that already exists in `audio_service.dart`); a **0-baseline ratchet** for raw `HapticFeedback.` so exactly these strays cannot return; and a *decision* — not a task — about whether grading belongs to the organic or the clean sound vocabulary **before** wiring accents into flashcard review, where `zen_bell_chime` also currently means both "session complete" and "lesson complete".


- [x] **The motion campaign turns to charm: a tap that bleeds ink, and three animations that teach (2026-09-26):** (1) **Reported:** *"are there any nice animation/cool and transition that could be added?"* and then *"try to do everything in tier 1 and tier 2 animations that teach"* — go past the compliance sweep and add delight, but only where it **teaches** or **responds to a touch**; (2) **The tap is ink now:** `ThemeData.splashFactory` had **never** been set (measured: **0** occurrences), so all **35** `InkWell`s plus the one `InkResponse` painted Flutter's grey disc while every other surface spoke ink and paper. New `lib/core/theme/zen_ink_splash.dart` backs `splashFactory:` in **both** themes, drawing a touch as a feathered, slightly irregular drop of ink (two sine lobes on the radius, radial-gradient edge) on `ZenMotion.quick`/`enter`, dissolving with `breathe`. `splashColor` *is* the tint and the factory thins it to **10%**, so the themes keep a full-strength accent — cinnabar on paper, amber on ink (cinnabar would be invisible on the dark surface); (3) **Three that teach:** the pronunciation contour now **draws itself** left to right (a `reveal` 0-1 on `_PitchContourPainter`, 96 even arc-length stations with a **lerped head** so the travelling tip is smooth), `ZenFlight.to()` carries a card **into the deck** from `deck_card_picker_screen` (choosing the site by reconnaissance: `word_detail_dialog` was **rejected** because its deck target is not on screen, so there is nothing honest to fly *to*), and `ZenAssembly` makes the radical forge show **how the character is built** — the one interaction in this app allowed to overshoot (`ZenMotion.arrival`); (4) **The two traps found and avoided:** the contour is fed *live* contours while the learner is still speaking, so the naive version would **strobe** — replay is gated on a genuinely new contour and a mid-flight update lets the current stroke finish (both pinned by tests); and the assembly reads the **real IDS decomposition already in `hanzi_metadata.json`** (`⿰日月` → 明), falling back to the honest two-element form when the metadata has none, **rather than fabricating components**; (5) **Reduce Motion:** all four collapse to no travel **and no ticker** — the ink is simply put down at a fixed tint, the contour is painted complete on the first frame, the radical is shown composed, and the flight is skipped entirely while still reporting arrival, so the add flow is unchanged for a user who asked for less motion; (6) **A blocker closed:** another agent had raised P2 *"`zen_ink_splash.dart` does not compile, blocks the build"* against this file while it was mid-edit, and correctly refused to guess at someone else's geometry. The report was right, and the symptom was the least of it: the whole clip was hand-rolled and wrong, and it was repaired against the SDK's own private helper (`clipOf` = Flutter's `_getClipCallback`) plus `getOuterPath` / `toRRect` / `clipRect` clipping over `canvas.transform(transform.storage)`. The dead helper was deleted and the ISSUES entry is now `[x]` with this as its evidence; (7) **Verification:** four suites **29/29** (`zen_ink_splash` **10**, `calligraphic_pitch_contour` **8** — an existing file extended rather than duplicated, `zen_flight` **5**, `zen_assembly` **6**); `motion_vocabulary` + `motion_guard` **16/16** with the seven ratchets **unmoved** — raw `Curves.` **1**, raw durations **121** ≤ 123 (measured **4 before and after** in `radical_lesson_screen`: another agent's `Future.delayed` hunk in the diff was a re-indent, not new timing), `.ms` **10**, `ZenMotion.` **290** ≥ 240; `flutter analyze lib` → **no errors** (of the 4 infos, 3 are pre-existing `curly_braces` in another agent's `conversation_screen.dart` and 1 was fixed in `smart_media_desk_screen`; the 2 infos this step introduced in its own test were fixed before reporting); (8) **Deliberately left:** the four remaining "cool but optional" ideas are queued in `ISSUES.md` — scroll unroll, book cover swing, deck riffle, ink drop for Ink Points, brush-stroke audiobook progress — and two of them **need a standards decision first**, because they change a token the vocabulary already ships (§ 2.1, *one value per interaction*), rather than quietly re-timing it.

- [x] **A reopened study session keeps the cards it already reserved (2026-09-25):** (1) **Reported:** *"I think there's an issue with the anki card, card to review today (or new card), it stop after only one card"*; (2) **Diagnosis:** `StudyQueueBuilder` dropped every card that was `isNew` **and** already in that mode's `modeIntroductionKeys` — a pair that can only match a card the learner never studied (reserving writes `introducedAt` and the activity sets, never `attempts`). - [x] **Deck session: "finishing a card" is one motion again (2026-09-24 04:20):** (1) **Reported:** *"during the deck: the animation when we finish a card is weird"*; (2) **Root cause:** the deck review session renders **one route per card** and grades via `Navigator.pop(context, grade)`, but the route underneath was the session's **"Ready to study" preview** (`study_session_preview` — a big *Start session* button + *Not now*) — so each finished card ran: swipe-off (stamp 130ms → 90ms pause → slide 180ms) → **full `MaterialPageRoute` page transition to the preview** → **another full page transition** into the next card. Between two words the learner saw a Start button, and on iOS the Cupertino builder slid the preview in from the left; (3) **Fix 1 (composition):** `_sessionStarted` gates the base surface — while a session runs the screen behind the cards is a deliberately empty paper surface (`_buildSessionSurface`), so a swap reveals nothing; the preview exists only before the first card; (4) **Fix 2 (motion):** new `_CardRoute extends PageRouteBuilder` — incoming sheet settles over `ZenMotion.quick` (300ms) with a 6% rise + fade on `ZenMotion.enter`, outgoing leg leaves on `ZenMotion.swap` (180ms), both `Duration.zero` + no travel under `context.reduceMotion` — replacing the per-card page transition with a card swap built only from existing motion tokens; (5) **Fix 3 (no dead time):** the SM-2 write + activity record used to be awaited *between* the pop and the push, which is precisely when the preview was visible; `_applyGrade` now books the card synchronously and chains persistence onto a `_writeChain` (chained, never parallel, so two cards cannot interleave a controller-state update) that runs alongside the next card, drained by `_drainPendingWrites()` before the summary and before popping an aborted session — no review can be lost; (6) **Fix 4 (latent bug found while testing):** `SwipeableFlashcard` did `await HapticsManager.medium()` **between** the stamp and the slide-off, and haptics ride `SystemChannels.platform`, which no `TestWidgetsFlutterBinding` answers — so in tests that await **never completed** and the card never advanced (no timer, so nothing failed loudly). The haptic is now `unawaited(...)`, consistent with the app's other 30 `unawaited` call sites; (7) **L10N bonus:** all four mode widgets hardcoded `"Swipe to Grade:"` and the `⬅️ Again ➡️ Good ⬆️ Easy ⬇️ Hard` line (English in 13 locales) — both now use the existing 14-locale keys `swipeToGrade`/`again`/`good`/`easy`/`hard`, patched by an ASCII-only script (arrows built from code points) that validates braces and refuses leftovers; (8) **Tests:** new `deck_review_session_transition_test.dart` (5) drives the real gesture (reveal → explicit `TestGesture` drag → grade) on a Hive-free stack (stub deck repo, stub activity repo, fake card controller, mocked `SharedPreferences` + mocked `SystemChannels.platform`) and asserts the preview is absent from the tree while a card is open and after a grade, that the next card is laid out mid-swap, that the backgrounded write still lands, plus two source guards; the earlier "weird" symptom was reproduced and every beat probed with temporary `debugPrint` markers (removed); (9) **Verification:** `flutter analyze lib` = **0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 infos**; `flutter test test/features/flashcards test/shared` = **139/139**.

the screen now shows a **mastery ring** (share in the centre, New/Learning/Mastered with counts *and* percentages), **accuracy by mode** (overall figure + a tinted bar per mode with a muted "—" for modes never practised), **seven-day workload** (`dueToday` + forecast bars labelled with the locale's narrow weekdays from its own first day of week), **momentum** (new this week / total reviews / average attempts per word) and the two word lists **Tricky characters** (most attempts, lowest accuracy) and **Strongest characters** (longest intervals, graduated only), each row showing hanzi + pinyin + meaning + its metric; (6) **Empty deck** gets an explained ink well instead of a wall of zeros; (7) **L10N:** added `trickyCharacters`, `strongestCharacters`, `newThisWeek`, `averageAttemptsPerWord`, `noCardsYet` to all **14** locales (validated-fragment insertion script + `flutter gen-l10n`) and reused existing 14-locale keys for mode names/accuracy/attempts/reviews/days/dayStreakCount/dueToday; - [x] **Article Simplification Repair — "Simplify failed: Simplification returned an empty article." (2026-09-23 15:42):** (1) **Reproduced live** against `deepseek/deepseek-chat` with a scratch harness (deleted afterwards): a 1,482-character Chinese article failed with *"Simplification dropped too much of the article."* on one run and `TimeoutException after 0:01:30` on the next, while a 1,238-character **English** page was silently accepted as a 2-sentence / 31-character "article"; (2) **Root cause #1 — no language gate:** `WebBrowserScreen` passes whatever `innerText` the selector found, so an English page reached a "rewrite this Chinese article" prompt that then announced *"the source has 0 Chinese characters and 0 sentences"* and asked for *"0 to 0 Chinese characters"* — an instruction to return nothing, which is how `{"sentences": []}` (and the reader's "empty article") is produced. `simplifyTextToHsk` now refuses a source below `minimumSourceChineseCharacters` (20) **before any HTTP call**, answering *"No Chinese article text was found on this page."*; (3) **Root cause #2 — brittle parsing:** any payload that was not a `sentences` array of `chinese`-keyed objects was discarded whole, including JSON merely cut off by the 8,192-token budget. New `_parseSimplifiedStory` (top-level array, nested `article`/`simplified`/`result`/`data`), `salvageSentences` (recovers every complete `"chinese": "..."` pair, each rebuilt with one word per character so it still renders), and `AiSentence.fromJson` alias support for `simplified`/`text`; (4) **Root cause #3 — an impossible ask:** the prompt demanded **2x the source length**, which blew the completion budget and the 90s timeout; it now asks the rewrite to *stay at least as long as the source*, omits the numeric rule where there is no measurable Chinese, and the call timeout is 150s; (5) **A shorter rewrite is no longer fatal:** 90% (`minimumRetryRatio`) still retries, the new `minimumAcceptableRatio` (**35%**, the value the app shipped before build 559) is the fatal floor, and `_ContentLossFormatException` (a `FormatException` subclass, so the screens still strip the type prefix) avoids a wasted third call; (6) **Chunked resilience:** `_simplifyChunkResiliently` retries a failed chunk once and reports it as missing; `simplifyTextToHsk` keeps every chunk that succeeded and fails only when nothing survived, reporting the real cause; chunks are now **700** characters (was 1,800) because the JSON is ~30x the Chinese character count (measured: a 504-character section finished at ~16k characters of JSON; an 875-character one was cut off at the cap; a 474-character section reached 111% coverage vs 16% for a 1,200-character one); (7) **Blank-paragraph fix:** `SimplifiedArticleReaderScreen` composes prose from `sentence.words`, so it now falls back to `sentence.chinese` for a sentence with no word breakdown; (8) **Verified after the fix:** the same 1,482-character article returns 9 sentences and the English page is refused locally with **zero** HTTP calls; new `test/core/services/simplify_resilience_test.dart` (7 tests) + `test/features/media/simplified_reader_word_fallback_test.dart` pass, and `gemini_service_simplification_test.dart` (3/3) / `simplify_length_preservation_test.dart` (3/3) still pass — 13/13 plus the guard; (9) **Hand-off:** the remaining failure mode is the open P1 upstream `429` / `503 UNAVAILABLE` with no retry or backoff (observed live), so a long article can come back shorter than the source — a lost chunk is logged and the others are still delivered.

- [x] **Reader notice lifecycle fixed (2026-09-23 19:00):** (1) **Root cause of the persistent dark banner:** it was a Material `SnackBar` from `_showResumeToastIfRestored()` (hardcoded English `'Resumed: Ch.4/120, Sent.1/228'`) owned by the **root** `ScaffoldMessenger` rather than the reader route, so dismissing the reader within its dwell left it on screen over the next screen — it was never an audio-service artifact; (2) **All four reader notices now use `ZenToast`** (root overlay, self-dismissing, tap-to-dismiss): resume = **info** with `chapterXOfY` · `sentenceXOfY`, playback failure = **error**, bookmark added = **success**, bookmark removed = **info** → the reader raises no `SnackBar` at all; (3) **Deferral tokenized:** `Future<void>.delayed(ZenMotion.quick, …)` replaces the raw `Duration(milliseconds: 600)`; (4) **Safety net:** `ScaffoldMessengerState` is captured in the new `didChangeDependencies` override and `clearSnackBars()` runs in `dispose()`; (5) **Audio audit — no defect found, no change needed:** `dispose()` already calls `audioServiceProvider.stop()` → `_stopEngines()` (stops `audioplayers` **and** `flutter_tts`; `_audioPlayer`/`_fallbackTts` are just the backing fields of the `_player`/`_tts` getters) and pauses the ambient soundscape; `AudiobookPlayerScreen.dispose()` mirrors it. - [x] **`StaggeredListItem` index-0 crash fixed (2026-09-23 16:05):** (1) **Root cause:** the stagger hold is `widget.delay * effectiveIndex`, so the *first* row has `holdMicros == 0`, and the hold was emitted as `TweenSequenceItem(weight: 0)` — `TweenSequenceItem` asserts `weight > 0.0`, so the row threw during `StatefulElement._firstBuild` (`#3 _StaggeredListItemState.initState (staggered_list_item.dart:56:7)` → `#2 new TweenSequenceItem (tween_sequence.dart:125:81)`), which is what made `CulturalContextScreen` (hero card = `StaggeredListItem(index: 0)`, line 74) fail to mount in tests; (2) **Fix:** the zero-length hold is omitted from both the fade and slide sequences (`if (holdMicros > 0) ...`), so index 0 and `delay: Duration.zero` mount and animate without a hold segment — behaviour for index > 0 is unchanged, and the four other call sites (`deck_detail_screen`, `dictionary_screen`, `book_detail_screen`, `cultural_context_screen`) are unaffected; (3) **Impact corrected:** asserts are compiled out of release builds (`flutter_tools.buildModeOptions` passes `--enable-asserts` for `BuildMode.debug` only), so TestFlight/App Store never crashed; `flutter run` (debug) and `flutter test` did; (4) **Verification:** new *staggered list item first row* group in `test/shared/motion_accessibility_test.dart` (index 0 mounts + settles fully opaque; zero delay mounts with no hold) — previously only indices 3/15/20 were covered, which is why the bug was latent; `test/shared`, `test/features/{flashcards,reading,media}`, `test/core`, `test/l10n` = **314 passed**; `flutter analyze lib` = **0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 infos**; (5) **Still open (needs a product decision, not a code fix):** 2 assertions in `cultural_context_book_parity_test.dart` describe the pre-refactor structure — `SliverAppBar` (the screen now uses a transparent `AppBar` over `Scaffold(backgroundColor: AppTheme.surfaceOf(context))`, matching `book_detail_screen.dart`) and the `ARTICLE OF THE DAY` badge (the refactored `_buildBadgeRow` renders `l10n.aiGenerated` + source host instead; the pill vocabulary itself is correct).

- [x] **Motion Slice P0: Reduced-Motion Coverage, Zen Motion Tokens & One Page Transition (2026-09-21 22:10):** (1) **Reduced motion was honoured by only 3 of 18 looping animations** (the prior motion task fixed only `ShimmerSkeleton`, `BreathingWidget` and `StreakFlameBadge`), leaving 14 perpetual `.repeat()` sites pulsing forever regardless of the OS setting; every one was moved out of `initState` (which cannot read `MediaQuery`) into `didChangeDependencies` and resolved through `MotionResolution`: `ai_progress_bar` (rests mid-bar at 0.5, now using `ZenMotion.ambient`), `radical_lesson_screen` (decorative ink sweep rests off-frame at 0.0 rather than frozen mid-flight), `discovery_step` (mid-glow 0.5), `live_call_screen` (`_pulseController` 0.0 = natural scale, `_analyzePulseController` 1.0), `media_search_screen` and `smart_media_desk_screen` (mid-grey 0.5), `nuance_compare_sheet` (caret holds full opacity at 1.0), and the three-dot `_InkDots` indicators duplicated in `ai_explainer_sheet` and `character_chat_sheet` (rest fully visible at 1.0, with the staggered `forward()` calls gated on a cached `_reduceMotion` flag); `premium_transcript_line` was routed through a single `_syncPulse()` helper so start/stop/`didUpdateWidget` all agree; `streak_seal` returns the static flame under reduced motion; `shadowing_studio_screen` only starts its pulse when allowed; (2) **Removed two dead perpetual tickers:** `travel_interpreter_screen` and `web_browser_screen` each created, repeated and disposed a `_pulseController` that no widget ever read (grep confirmed exactly three references each: declaration, init, dispose) — pure per-frame work for no visual effect; (3) **`ZenMotion` tokens** (`lib/core/theme/zen_motion.dart`, new): single source of truth for the mandated feel — `quick` 300ms, `swap` 180ms, `page` 500ms, `forStrokes(n)` = strokes * 800ms, `ambient`, plus `natural` (`Curves.easeInOutQuart`) and `arrival` (`easeOutBack`); the curve alone previously appeared as 26 scattered literals; (4) **One page transition for the whole app:** added `ZenPageTransitionsBuilder` (cross-fade + 3% lift, collapsing to fade-only under reduced motion) and registered it in **both** themes for Android/Windows/Linux/Fuchsia, replacing the dated `FadeUpwardsPageTransitionsBuilder`; because `SwipeBackRoute` is a `MaterialPageRoute` subclass with no custom `transitionsBuilder`, this one theme change restyles **all 89 route call-sites** (66 `SwipeBackPageRoute` + 23 bare `MaterialPageRoute`) without editing a single call-site; iOS/macOS deliberately keep `CupertinoPageTransitionsBuilder` because it supplies the interactive edge-swipe back gesture; (5) **Guard rails:** new `test/core/motion_guard_test.dart` (7/7 pass) asserting the token contract against `docs/UI_UX_STANDARDS.md`, that both themes share the Zen transition, that every `.repeat()` site consults `context.reduceMotion`/`MotionResolution`, that the dated transition is gone (comment-stripped source so doc references don't false-positive), and ratcheting bare `MaterialPageRoute` at a baseline of 23 — plus two runtime proofs that a perpetual sweep schedules **zero transient frame callbacks** under reduced motion and does schedule them when motion is allowed; (6) **Docs:** rewrote "Animations & Motion" in `docs/UI_UX_STANDARDS.md` around `ZenMotion` + the reduced-motion rule and added four motion anti-patterns to `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md`; (7) **Verification:** `test/core/motion_guard_test.dart` 7/7; full `flutter test` -> **568 passed / 4 failed**, with all 4 failures pre-existing and unrelated (proved earlier on a pristine `HEAD` worktree: `book_download_service` (network), `poetry_cover_manifest` (asset licensing data), `stroke_matcher` x2 (pure math)) — zero regressions from the page-transition or loop changes. (Status: COMPLETED)


- [x] **Motion System: Reduced-Motion Accessibility, Hero Transitions & Shared Animation Widgets (2026-09-21 21:05):** (1) **Reduced-motion accessibility (previously 0 support app-wide):** added `MotionPreferences` (`lib/shared/utils/motion_preferences.dart`) with a `context.reduceMotion` extension over `MediaQueryData.disableAnimations` plus a `MotionResolution` helper that starts/freezes/settles a controller to a static value; fixed the three **perpetual** loops that ignored the OS setting — `ShimmerSkeleton` (`repeat(reverse: true)`), `BreathingWidget`, and `StreakFlameBadge` — each now rests at a static mid-value so the placeholder/glow stays visible without pulsing (accessibility correctness for vestibular disorders); `HeroTransition.wrap`, `ZenFilterPill`, `LoadingSwap` and `StaggeredListItem` all collapse to instant state changes; (2) **Shared-element (Hero) transitions, previously absent from every primary navigation path:** added `HeroTransition` (`lib/shared/utils/hero_transition.dart`) with **namespaced tags** (`'<scope>::<id>'`) — the critical safety detail, since `Hero` tags must be unique per route and shelf/catalog screens render the same card in multiple rails (horizontal shelf, "continue reading", category grid), so a naive tag would collide and throw at runtime; wired **Book catalog → book detail** (`CalligraphicBookCover` flies grid cell → 135×190 header), **Deck library → deck preview sheet** (`CalligraphicDeckCover` via `showModalBottomSheet`), and **Show catalog → show detail** (thumbnail expands into the full-bleed backdrop); `flightShuttleBuilder` renders the destination child mid-flight so source chrome (bookmark badges, "SAVED" ribbons, CC chips) never flashes; (3) **Unified filter pills:** `ChoiceChip` in `scenario_selection_screen` and `story_library_screen` (category + HSK rows) snapped colour in a single frame while `tome_manager_screen` already tweened 180ms — extracted the canonical pill as `ZenFilterPill` (`lib/shared/widgets/zen_filter_pill.dart`) and adopted it in all three screens with haptics; (4) **`LoadingSwap`** (`lib/shared/widgets/loading_swap.dart`) cross-fades a button icon into a spinner with a constant-footprint `layoutBuilder` (prevents `Row` jitter), replacing three snap `if/else` swaps in `web_browser_screen`; (5) **Bug fixed — staggered list content could be stranded invisible:** `StaggeredListItem` drove its entrance with a bare `Future.delayed`, whose callback is **not** ticker-driven, so when it did not run (disposed mid-delay on a fast scroll, or a host settling the animation clock without draining pending timers) the item was left at **opacity 0.0 — permanently invisible row content**; an intermediate `Timer`+`dispose` cancellation fixed the double-animation flash but not the stranding; rewrote the delay as a **hold at the head of a `TweenSequence`** inside the controller timeline so the animation is the single source of truth and full opacity is always reached (orphaned-callback bugs now impossible by construction); applied it to the previously static chapter list in `book_detail_screen`; (6) **Verification:** new suite `test/shared/motion_accessibility_test.dart` (15 tests) including the "always reaches full opacity, never stranding content" invariant, plus a documented pitfall that `pumpAndSettle` times out against `CircularProgressIndicator` (fixed assertions use `pump(duration)`); `flutter test test/shared/ …` = **41/41 passed**; `flutter analyze` (whole project) = **No issues found! (0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 infos)**. Build `1.0.0+558`.

(4) **Localization:** replaced the hardcoded `'Report'` in `pronunciation_report_sheet.dart` with the existing `l10n.report`; (5) **New guard rails:** `test/support/locale_layout_harness.dart` (worst-case locale x viewport x text-scale sweep whose `expectNoOverflow()` *fails* on `RenderFlex overflowed` instead of swallowing it) and `test/core/locale_layout_guard_test.dart` (bans `fixedSize` on buttons, requires an `overflow:`/`maxLines:` strategy on any fixed-width `Text`, ratchets untranslated UI literals at a baseline of 81, and asserts the button geometry contract) — 5/5 pass, and the harness immediately caught a real Vietnamese 2.0x overflow during development (English "Report" -> "Báo cáo chi tiết", 2.7x); two doubly-encoded comment separators (`media_search_screen.dart:740,744`) are tracked in `ISSUES.md` as cosmetic; (7) **Docs:** `docs/UI_UX_STANDARDS.md` gained a "Localization Layout Budget" section, `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md` four new anti-patterns, and `docs/LOCALIZATION_PIPELINE.md` a Step 5 sweep plus a `scratch/` tooling table; - [x] **Nuance Comparison Sheet: Empty Chat History & Corrupted Output Fix (2026-09-21 20:15):** (1) **"Continuer la discussion" opened an empty, amnesiac chat:** diagnosed `_enterChatMode()` in `lib/shared/widgets/nuance_compare_sheet.dart` — it constructed a brand-new `AiChatSession` via `startCharacterChat()` and never seeded `_chatMessages` (which only ever grew from `_sendChatMessage()`), so the panel opened blank and the AI had no memory of the comparison being continued; the same method also hardcoded `'en'` instead of the app locale. Fixed by seeding `_chatMessages` with `_streamedText` (guarded by `isNotEmpty` + `isEmpty` so empty/errored results and re-entries never duplicate), scrolling to bottom on entry, passing `Localizations.localeOf(context).languageCode`, and adding an `isContext` flag to `_ChatMessage` with a dedicated `_buildChatView()` branch that renders the carried-over comparison as formatted reference content under a labelled divider rather than as an AI bubble; (2) **"Weird" comparison output:** two root causes in `_buildFormattedContent` — `PinyinUtils.convertNumericToMarks()` was applied to the *entire* AI response (rewriting digits inside ordinary English prose and headings) and the highlight loop mutated `block` while iterating `widget.words` (making which word got bolded depend on iteration order). Fixed with a Han-gated `_normalizePinyin()` helper (`[\u4e00-\u9fff]` check, blocks split from untouched `rawText`) and an order-independent `_leadingWordOf()` helper that resolves the target once from the raw line and applies a single `RegExp.escape`d replacement across `*`/`-`/`•` bullet forms; (3) **Localized** the new `comparisonLabel` key across all 14 `.arb` files and regenerated `lib/l10n/app_localizations*.dart`; (4) **Verification:** new source-based regression suite `test/shared/nuance_compare_sheet_test.dart` (11 tests) — confirmed it genuinely fails (2 failures) when the formatting fix is reverted, then restored the fix; `flutter test` across the nuance sheet + deck picker + QA localization suites **33/33 passed**; `flutter analyze` on both changed files reports **No issues found!** Build `1.0.0+558`.

    - Fixed the 5-second "reconnecting" watchdog loop by extending listening duration to 60s, increasing silence pause tolerance to 3s, and silently maintaining the listening session.
    - Wired live `_toggleSpeaker()` to native audio session category routing.

    - Extracted and populated `expectedTone` and `actualTone` for pronunciation feedback chips.

Added a proper fallback UI displaying a permission request and an "Open Settings" button, and wrapped the background to prevent it from shrinking to 0x0 size and showing a blank light gray screen that hides the white navigation icons.

Replaced the outdated beige error banner with a premium, floating toast notification that correctly guides users to settings if permission is permanently denied.
- [x] **Universal Scanner:** Fixed a severe UI distortion bug in landscape mode caused by hardcoded camera preview aspect ratio dimensions. The scanner now dynamically calculates aspect ratios using `math.max` and `math.min` to flawlessly adapt to any screen rotation.

Implemented a robust manual iterator pattern to gracefully catch and skip individual items with parsing errors while keeping the rest of the search results intact.

Rewrote `translateTranscriptLines` to use **chunked processing** (20 lines/batch) to fix silent failures on long transcripts. Added Pinyin auto-detection to correctly populate the `pinyin` field when YouTube serves Pinyin-only captions. Renamed toggle to "Show Translation".
- [x] **Task 24: Smart Media Desk â€” Pinyinâ†’Hanzi & Performance Fix**: Fixed 3 root causes: (1) Track selection now uses `isAutoGenerated` flag to prefer manual Hanzi caption tracks over YouTube's auto-generated Pinyin ones. (2) Translation pipeline changed from "wait for all chunks â†’ one UI update" to **incremental chunk-by-chunk UI updates** so Hanzi appears progressively. (3) Sync engine throttled from 60fps to 4Hz (`setState` max every 250ms) to eliminate jank and slow button taps. Added `translateChunk()` to `GeminiService` for single-chunk processing.

(2) QuickLook buttons less cramped: height 52â†’46, added icons, smaller font. (3) Fullscreen overlay button (â›¶) on video. Re-engineered `_localSearchFallback` to default to pre-validated `HardcodedShows` videos when exact category tags (e.g. vlog, gaming) don't match, ensuring users always see content. Implemented dynamic live key rotation across all YouTube endpoints to cycle through backup API keys on 403 Quota Exceeded errors so fetching remains functional.

- [x] **Task 31: Gemini Live Call â€” Echo & Transcript Polish**: Resolved severe echo and self-interruption loops by enabling OS hardware echo cancellation. Re-enabled invisible AI transcripts by correctly passing `["TEXT", "AUDIO"]` modalities. Formatted AI responses via `TappableMarkdownHanziText` for instant QuickLook access.
- [x] **Task 32: iOS Build Fix**: Fixed Dart syntax errors (missing parenthesis and invalid argument name) in `conversation_screen.dart` that were crashing the kernel snapshot compiler during iOS archiving.
- [x] **Task 33: Shadowing Studio Grading Fix**: Fixed an issue where the pronunciation grading always returned `0/100`. The Azure Speech Pronunciation Assessment API was failing to align complete conversational sentences due to Chinese punctuation (like `ã€‚`, `ï¼`). Stripped all punctuation from the `ReferenceText` payload via Unicode Regex `[^\p{Script=Hani}a-zA-Z0-9 ]` to ensure flawless phoneme alignment.
- [x] **Task 34: Gemini Live Call Fix**: Fixed a WebSocket 1007 crash loop by removing the unsupported `"TEXT"` modality from the API payload. Also implemented friendly error states to catch 1000-level disconnects gracefully, and added a styled escape button for fallback UI scenarios.

Fixed by calling `GlobalDictionaryRepository.getExact(hanzi)` for every word before saving, hydrating `pinyin`, `definition`, and `hskLevel` from the bundled SQLite dictionary. AI grading pinyin is kept as a secondary fallback.
